﻿// File: \ViewModels\CompositionRow.cs
namespace Panex.ViewModels;

public class CompositionRow
{
    public string Symbol { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    public int Z { get; set; }
    public double AtomicMass { get; set; }
    public double AtomFraction { get; set; }
    public double MassFraction { get; set; }

    // WinUI 3 DataGrid Formatting Helpers
    public string AtomicMassString => AtomicMass.ToString("F4");
    public string AtomFractionString => AtomFraction.ToString("P4");
    public string MassFractionString => MassFraction.ToString("P4");
} 
===== ..\Panex\ViewModels\CurveStyle.cs =====
﻿// File: \ViewModels\CurveStyle.cs
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using System;

namespace Panex.ViewModels
{
    public partial class CurveStyle : ObservableObject
    {
        private readonly Action _repaint;

        // Default states for resetting
        private readonly Windows.UI.Color _defaultColor;
        private readonly ScottPlot.LinePattern _defaultPattern;
        private readonly double _defaultLineWidth;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string name = string.Empty;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private Windows.UI.Color uiColor;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private ScottPlot.LinePattern selectedPattern;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private double lineWidth;

        // Valid ScottPlot 5 Line Patterns
        public ScottPlot.LinePattern[] AvailablePatterns { get; } = new[]
        {
            ScottPlot.LinePattern.Solid,
            ScottPlot.LinePattern.Dashed,
            ScottPlot.LinePattern.Dotted,
            ScottPlot.LinePattern.DenselyDashed,
        };

        // Helper property to safely translate Windows.UI.Color to ScottPlot.Color
        public ScottPlot.Color SpColor => ScottPlot.Color.FromARGB((uint)((UiColor.A << 24) | (UiColor.R << 16) | (UiColor.G << 8) | UiColor.B));

        public CurveStyle(string name, Windows.UI.Color defaultColor, ScottPlot.LinePattern defaultPattern, Action repaint)
        {
            Name = name;
            _repaint = repaint;

            // Store the initial baseline defaults so they can be restored later
            _defaultColor = defaultColor;
            _defaultPattern = defaultPattern;

            // CHANGE THIS TO 2.0
            _defaultLineWidth = 2.0;

            // Initialize the active properties
            UiColor = defaultColor;
            SelectedPattern = defaultPattern;
            LineWidth = _defaultLineWidth;
        }

        public void ResetToDefaults()
        {
            UiColor = _defaultColor;
            SelectedPattern = _defaultPattern;
            LineWidth = _defaultLineWidth;
        }

        // Trigger the debounce timer whenever a visual property changes
        partial void OnUiColorChanged(Windows.UI.Color value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnSelectedPatternChanged(ScottPlot.LinePattern value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnLineWidthChanged(double value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
    }
} 
===== ..\Panex\ViewModels\DataTableViewModel.cs =====
﻿// File: \ViewModels\DataTableViewModel.cs
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Panex.Models;
using Panex.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer;

namespace Panex.ViewModels;

public partial class DataTableViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    private readonly PeriodicTableService _ptService;
    private readonly BuildupCalculator _buildupCalc;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? crossSectionData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? crossSectionHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? macData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? macHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? lacData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? lacHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? depthsData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? depthsHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? zeffData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? zeffHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? buildupData;
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? buildupHeaders;

    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<AbsorptionEdgeRow>? absorptionEdgesData;

    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _crossSectionCache = new();
    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _macCache = new();
    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _lacCache = new();
    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _depthsCache = new();
    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _zeffCache = new();
    private Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> _buildupCache = new();

    public DataTableViewModel(PeriodicTableService ptService, BuildupCalculator buildupCalc)
    {
        _ptService = ptService;
        _buildupCalc = buildupCalc;
    }

    public void ClearCaches()
    {
        _crossSectionCache.Clear();
        _macCache.Clear();
        _lacCache.Clear();
        _depthsCache.Clear();
        _zeffCache.Clear();
        _buildupCache.Clear();
    }

    private void BuildTableGeneric(
        Material material,
        IEnumerable<double> energyGrid,
        ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> headers,
        Func<double, IEnumerable<string>> rowGenerator,
        Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow> Data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> Headers)> cache,
        Action<ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>> setDataSet,
        Action<ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>> setHeaders)
    {
        if (material == null) return;

        if (cache.TryGetValue(material, out var cached))
        {
            setHeaders(cached.Headers);
            setDataSet(cached.Data);
            return;
        }

        var dataList = new ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>();
        string lastEnergyString = null; // Tracks the previous row's energy

        foreach (var e in energyGrid)
        {
            string currentEnergyString = e.ToString("G5");

            // Visually deduplicate: If it rounds to the exact same string as the last row, skip it.
            if (currentEnergyString == lastEnergyString) continue;
            lastEnergyString = currentEnergyString;

            dataList.Add(new PhysicsTableRow
            {
                Energy = currentEnergyString,
                Values = rowGenerator(e).ToList()
            });
        }

        cache[material] = (dataList, headers);
        setHeaders(headers);
        setDataSet(dataList);
    }

    private void BuildComparisonTableGeneric(
        IEnumerable<Material> materials,
        IEnumerable<double> energyGrid,
        Func<Material, double, string> valueSelector,
        Action<ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>> setDataSet,
        Action<ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>> setHeaders,
        string unitHeader)
    {
        if (materials == null || !materials.Any())
        {
            setHeaders(new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>());
            setDataSet(new ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>());
            return;
        }

        var headers = new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> { new ColumnHeader { Title = "Energy (MeV)" } };
        foreach (var mat in materials)
        {
            headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = string.IsNullOrEmpty(unitHeader) ? mat.Name : $"{mat.Name} ({unitHeader})" });
        }

        var dataList = new ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>();
        string lastEnergyString = null; // Tracks the previous row's energy

        foreach (var e in energyGrid)
        {
            string currentEnergyString = e.ToString("G5");

            // Visually deduplicate for the comparison table as well
            if (currentEnergyString == lastEnergyString) continue;
            lastEnergyString = currentEnergyString;

            var rowVals = new List<string> { currentEnergyString };
            foreach (var mat in materials)
            {
                rowVals.Add(valueSelector(mat, e));
            }
            dataList.Add(new PhysicsTableRow { Energy = currentEnergyString, Values = rowVals });
        }

        setHeaders(headers);
        setDataSet(dataList);
    }

    public void UpdateCrossSectionComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, List<double> energies, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn) =>
        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, energies, (mat, e) => mat.GetCustomTotalCrossSection(e, incPe, incCoh, incInc, incPair, incTrip, incPn).ToString("G6"), v => CrossSectionData = v, c => CrossSectionHeaders = c, "b");

    public void UpdateMacComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, List<double> energies, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn) =>
        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, energies, (mat, e) => (mat.GetCustomTotalCrossSection(e, incPe, incCoh, incInc, incPair, incTrip, incPn) * mat.MassAttenuationFactor).ToString("G6"), v => MacData = v, c => MacHeaders = c, "cm²/g");

    public void UpdateLacComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, List<double> energies, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn) =>
        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, energies, (mat, e) => (mat.Density == null || mat.Density <= 0) ? "N/A" : (mat.GetCustomTotalCrossSection(e, incPe, incCoh, incInc, incPair, incTrip, incPn) * mat.MassAttenuationFactor * mat.Density.Value).ToString("G6"), v => LacData = v, c => LacHeaders = c, "cm⁻¹");

    public void UpdateDepthsComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, List<double> energies, double nValue, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn)
    {
        string unitHeader = nValue == 1.0 ? "MFP (cm)" : (nValue == 2.0 ? "HVL (cm)" : (nValue == 10.0 ? "TVL (cm)" : $"1/{nValue:0.##} VL (cm)"));
        double logN = Math.Log(nValue);

        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, energies, (mat, e) =>
        {
            if (mat.Density == null || mat.Density <= 0) return "N/A";
            double lac = mat.GetCustomTotalCrossSection(e, incPe, incCoh, incInc, incPair, incTrip, incPn) * mat.MassAttenuationFactor * mat.Density.Value;
            if (lac <= 0) return "Infinity";
            return (nValue <= 1.0 ? 1.0 / lac : logN / lac).ToString("G6");
        }, v => DepthsData = v, c => DepthsHeaders = c, unitHeader);
    }

    public void UpdateZeffComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, List<double> energies, bool useInterpolated, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn)
    {
        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, energies, (mat, e) =>
        {
            if (!useInterpolated)
            {
                double sigmaAtomic = 0.0, sigmaElectronic = 0.0;
                foreach (var kvp in mat.Composition)
                {
                    var el = kvp.Key;
                    double fraction = kvp.Value;
                    double sig_i = 0.0;
                    if (incCoh) sig_i += el.GetPhotoatomicCurve(71, 0)?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0;
                    if (incInc) sig_i += el.GetPhotoatomicCurve(72, 0)?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0;
                    if (incPe) sig_i += el.GetPhotoatomicCurve(73, 0)?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0;
                    if (incPair) sig_i += el.GetPhotoatomicCurve(74, 0)?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0;
                    if (incTrip) sig_i += el.GetPhotoatomicCurve(75, 0)?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0;
                    if (incPn) sig_i += el.GetElementalPhotonuclearCurve().Interpolate(e);

                    sigmaAtomic += fraction * sig_i;
                    if (el.Z > 0) sigmaElectronic += fraction * (sig_i / el.Z);
                }
                return (sigmaElectronic > 0 ? sigmaAtomic / sigmaElectronic : 0.0).ToString("G6");
            }
            else
            {
                double st = mat.GetCrossSection(e);
                double directZeff = mat.GetEffectiveAtomicNumber(e);
                int minZ = mat.Composition.Keys.Min(el => el.Z);
                int maxZ = mat.Composition.Keys.Max(el => el.Z);
                double interpZeff = _ptService.CalculateInterpolatedZeff(e, st, minZ, maxZ, directZeff);
                return interpZeff > 0 ? interpZeff.ToString("G6") : "N/A";
            }
        }, v => ZeffData = v, c => ZeffHeaders = c, "");
    }

    public void UpdateBuildupComparisonTable(IEnumerable<Material> materials, double targetMfp, BuildupFactorType type)
    {
        BuildComparisonTableGeneric(materials, BuildupCalculator.AnsiEnergiesMeV, (mat, e) =>
        {
            double zeq = _buildupCalc.GetEquivalentAtomicNumber(mat, e);
            var gp = _buildupCalc.GetGpParameters(mat, e, type);
            if (gp != null && zeq > 0 && gp.A != 0)
            {
                double bf = _buildupCalc.CalculateBuildupFactor(mat, e, targetMfp, type);
                return bf >= 1.0 ? bf.ToString("G6") : "-";
            }
            return "-";
        }, v => BuildupData = v, c => BuildupHeaders = c, $"BF at {targetMfp} MFP");
    }

    private CrossSectionCurve? GetCurveAsObject((double[] Energies, double[] CrossSections) tuple) =>
        (tuple.Energies == null || tuple.Energies.Length == 0) ? null : new CrossSectionCurve(0, 0, 0, tuple.Energies, tuple.CrossSections);

    public void UpdateCrossSectionDataTable(Material material, List<double> energies, bool sTot, bool sCoh, bool sInc, bool sPe, bool sPairN, bool sPairE, bool sPn, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> isos, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> subs) =>
        UpdateStandardPhysicsTable("b", 1.0, material, energies, sTot, sCoh, sInc, sPe, sPairN, sPairE, sPn, isos, subs, _crossSectionCache, v => CrossSectionData = v, c => CrossSectionHeaders = c);

    public void UpdateMacDataTable(Material material, List<double> energies, bool sTot, bool sCoh, bool sInc, bool sPe, bool sPairN, bool sPairE, bool sPn, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> isos, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> subs) =>
        UpdateStandardPhysicsTable("cm²/g", material.MassAttenuationFactor, material, energies, sTot, sCoh, sInc, sPe, sPairN, sPairE, sPn, isos, subs, _macCache, v => MacData = v, c => MacHeaders = c);

    public void UpdateLacDataTable(Material material, List<double> energies, bool sTot, bool sCoh, bool sInc, bool sPe, bool sPairN, bool sPairE, bool sPn, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> isos, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> subs) =>
        UpdateStandardPhysicsTable("cm⁻¹", material.MassAttenuationFactor * (material.Density ?? 0.0), material, energies, sTot, sCoh, sInc, sPe, sPairN, sPairE, sPn, isos, subs, _lacCache, v => LacData = v, c => LacHeaders = c);

    private void UpdateStandardPhysicsTable(string unit, double mult, Material material, List<double> energies, bool sTot, bool sCoh, bool sInc, bool sPe, bool sPairN, bool sPairE, bool sPn, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> isos, ObservableCollection<SelectableItem> subs, Dictionary<Material, (ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>)> cache, Action<ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>> setDataSet, Action<ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>> setHeaders)
    {
        var headers = new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader> { new ColumnHeader { Title = "Energy (MeV)" } };
        if (sTot) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Total ({unit})" });
        if (sCoh) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Coherent ({unit})" });
        if (sInc) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Incoherent ({unit})" });
        if (sPe) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Photoelectric ({unit})" });
        if (sPairN) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Pair (Nuclear) ({unit})" });
        if (sPairE) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Pair (Electron) ({unit})" });
        if (sPn) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"Photonuclear ({unit})" });

        var activeIsotopes = isos.Where(i => i.IsSelected).ToList();
        var activeSubshells = subs.Where(s => s.IsSelected).ToList();

        foreach (var i in activeIsotopes) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = i.Name });
        foreach (var s in activeSubshells) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = s.Name });

        var totalC = sTot ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetTotalCrossSectionCurve()) : null;
        var cohC = sCoh ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetCrossSectionCurve(71, 0)) : null;
        var incohC = sInc ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetCrossSectionCurve(72, 0)) : null;
        var peC = sPe ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetCrossSectionCurve(73, 0)) : null;
        var pairC = sPairN ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetCrossSectionCurve(74, 0)) : null;
        var tripC = sPairE ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetCrossSectionCurve(75, 0)) : null;
        var pnC = sPn ? GetCurveAsObject(material.GetPhotonuclearCrossSectionCurve()) : null;

        BuildTableGeneric(material, energies, headers, (e) =>
        {
            var vals = new List<string> { e.ToString("G5") };
            if (sTot) vals.Add(((totalC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sCoh) vals.Add(((cohC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sInc) vals.Add(((incohC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sPe) vals.Add(((peC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sPairN) vals.Add(((pairC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sPairE) vals.Add(((tripC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));
            if (sPn) vals.Add(((pnC?.Interpolate(e) ?? 0.0) * mult).ToString("G6"));

            foreach (var a in activeIsotopes)
            {
                var el = material.Composition.Keys.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Z == a.Z);
                var iso = el?.Isotopes.FirstOrDefault(x => x.A == a.A);
                vals.Add(((el != null && iso != null) ? iso.GetPhotonuclearCrossSection(e) * iso.Abundance * material.Composition[el] * mult : 0.0).ToString("G6"));
            }
            foreach (var s in activeSubshells)
            {
                var el = material.Composition.Keys.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Z == s.Z);
                var curve = el?.GetPhotoatomicCurve(73, 91, s.SubshellIndex);
                vals.Add(((el != null && curve != null) ? curve.Interpolate(e) * material.Composition[el] * mult : 0.0).ToString("G6"));
            }
            return vals;
        }, cache, setDataSet, setHeaders);
    }

    public void UpdateZeffDataTable(Material material, List<double> energies)
    {
        var headers = new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>
        {
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Energy (MeV)" },
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Zeff (Direct)" },
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Zeff (Interpolated)" }
        };

        var zeffCurve = material.GetEffectiveAtomicNumberCurve();
        var zeffObject = new CrossSectionCurve(0, 0, 0, zeffCurve.Energies, zeffCurve.ZeffValues);
        var totalC = material.GetTotalCrossSectionCurve();
        var totalXsObject = new CrossSectionCurve(0, 0, 0, totalC.Energies, totalC.CrossSections);
        int minZ = material.Composition.Keys.Min(e => e.Z);
        int maxZ = material.Composition.Keys.Max(e => e.Z);

        BuildTableGeneric(material, energies, headers, (e) =>
        {
            var vals = new List<string> { e.ToString("G5") };
            double directZeff = zeffObject.Interpolate(e);
            vals.Add(directZeff.ToString("G6"));
            double st = totalXsObject.Interpolate(e);
            double interpZeff = _ptService.CalculateInterpolatedZeff(e, st, minZ, maxZ, directZeff);
            vals.Add(interpZeff > 0 ? interpZeff.ToString("G6") : "N/A");
            return vals;
        }, _zeffCache, v => ZeffData = v, c => ZeffHeaders = c);
    }

    public void UpdateDepthsDataTable(Material material, List<double> energies, double nValue, bool incPe, bool incCoh, bool incInc, bool incPair, bool incTrip, bool incPn)
    {
        var headers = new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>
        {
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Energy (MeV)" },
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "MFP (cm)" }
        };

        bool showNvl = nValue > 1;
        if (showNvl)
        {
            headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = nValue switch { 2.0 => "HVL (cm)", 4.0 => "QVL (cm)", 10.0 => "TVL (cm)", _ => $"1/{nValue:0.##} VL (cm)" } });
        }

        double factor = material.MassAttenuationFactor * (material.Density ?? 0.0);

        BuildTableGeneric(material, energies, headers, (e) =>
        {
            var vals = new List<string> { e.ToString("G5") };
            double sumXs = 0;
            if (incCoh) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 71);
            if (incInc) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 72);
            if (incPe) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 73);
            if (incPair) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 74);
            if (incTrip) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 75);
            if (incPn) sumXs += material.GetComponentCrossSection(e, 0);

            double customLac = sumXs * factor;
            vals.Add(customLac > 0 ? (1.0 / customLac).ToString("G6") : "Infinity");
            if (showNvl) vals.Add(customLac > 0 ? (Math.Log(nValue) / customLac).ToString("G6") : "Infinity");
            return vals;
        }, _depthsCache, v => DepthsData = v, c => DepthsHeaders = c);
    }

    public void UpdateBuildupDataTable(Material material, double[] targetMfps, BuildupFactorType type)
    {
        var headers = new ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>
        {
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Energy (MeV)" },
            new ColumnHeader { Title = "Zeq" }
        };
        foreach (var mfp in targetMfps) headers.Add(new ColumnHeader { Title = $"{mfp} MFP" });

        BuildTableGeneric(material, BuildupCalculator.AnsiEnergiesMeV, headers, (e) =>
        {
            var vals = new List<string> { e.ToString("G5") };
            double zeq = _buildupCalc.GetEquivalentAtomicNumber(material, e);
            vals.Add(zeq > 0 ? zeq.ToString("G6") : "N/A");

            var gp = _buildupCalc.GetGpParameters(material, e, type);
            foreach (var mfp in targetMfps)
            {
                if (gp != null && gp.A != 0 && zeq > 0) vals.Add(_buildupCalc.CalculateBuildupFactor(material, e, mfp, type).ToString("G6"));
                else vals.Add("-");
            }
            return vals;
        }, _buildupCache, v => BuildupData = v, c => BuildupHeaders = c);
    }

    public void UpdateAbsorptionEdgesTable(Material material)
    {
        if (material == null) { AbsorptionEdgesData = null; return; }
        var list = new List<AbsorptionEdgeRow>();
        foreach (var kvp in material.Composition) list.AddRange(kvp.Key.AbsorptionEdges);
        AbsorptionEdgesData = new ObservableCollection<AbsorptionEdgeRow>(list.OrderByDescending(x => x.EnergyMeV));
    }

    [RelayCommand] private void CopyCrossSectionTable() => CopyToClipboard(CrossSectionData, CrossSectionHeaders);
    [RelayCommand] private void CopyMacTable() => CopyToClipboard(MacData, MacHeaders);
    [RelayCommand] private void CopyLacTable() => CopyToClipboard(LacData, LacHeaders);
    [RelayCommand] private void CopyDepthsTable() => CopyToClipboard(DepthsData, DepthsHeaders);
    [RelayCommand] private void CopyZeffTable() => CopyToClipboard(ZeffData, ZeffHeaders);
    [RelayCommand] private void CopyBuildupTable() => CopyToClipboard(BuildupData, BuildupHeaders);

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyAbsorptionEdgesTable()
    {
        if (AbsorptionEdgesData == null || AbsorptionEdgesData.Count == 0) return;
        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("Element\tSubshell\tEnergy (keV)\tEnergy (MeV)");
        foreach (var row in AbsorptionEdgesData) sb.AppendLine($"{row.Element}\t{row.Subshell}\t{row.EnergyKeVString}\t{row.EnergyMeVString}");
        var dp = new DataPackage();
        dp.SetText(sb.ToString());
        Clipboard.SetContent(dp);
    }

    private void CopyToClipboard(ObservableCollection<PhysicsTableRow>? data, ObservableCollection<ColumnHeader>? headers)
    {
        if (data == null || headers == null || headers.Count == 0) return;
        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine(string.Join("\t", headers.Select(c => c.Title)));
        foreach (var row in data) sb.AppendLine(string.Join("\t", row.Values));
        var dp = new DataPackage();
        dp.SetText(sb.ToString());
        Clipboard.SetContent(dp);
    }
}

    {
        get => ChartVM.IsDarkMode;
        set { ChartVM.IsDarkMode = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
    }

    partial void OnShowChartChanged(bool value)
    {
        if (value) { ShowGrid = false; RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty); }
    }
    partial void OnShowGridChanged(bool value)
    {
        if (value)
        {
            ShowChart = false;
            if (IsZeqMode) IsEbfMode = true;
            UpdateTables();
        }
    }

    partial void OnIsCompareModeChanged(bool value)
    {
        if (value) // ENTERING COMPARE MODE
        {
            foreach (var mat in Materials)
            {
                mat.IsCompared = true;
            }

            // 1. Save the full list to memory before we ruin it
            _mfpMemory = TargetMfpInput;

            // 2. Truncate the input to just the first value
            if (TargetMfps.Length > 1)
            {
                TargetMfpInput = TargetMfps.FirstOrDefault().ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
            }
        }
        else // EXITING COMPARE MODE
        {
            // Restore the full list from memory
            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(_mfpMemory))
            {
                TargetMfpInput = _mfpMemory;
            }
        }

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowMainContent));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowEmptyState));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasValidDensity));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasBuildupFactors));

        ValidateCurrentTab();

        RefreshUIOnly();
    }
    #endregion

    #region Chart Settings States (Forwarded to ChartVM)
    [ObservableProperty] private PlotSettings currentPlotSettings;
    [ObservableProperty] private int selectedMainTabIndex = 0;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool isBuildupPlotActive = true;

    // Passthrough to ChartVM
    public PlotSettings CrossSectionSettings => ChartVM.CrossSectionSettings;
    public PlotSettings MacSettings => ChartVM.MacSettings;
    public PlotSettings LacSettings => ChartVM.LacSettings;

    public PlotSettings ZeqSettings => ChartVM.ZeqSettings;
    public Dictionary<PlotSettings, List<HoverCurveCache>> HoverDataMap => ChartVM.HoverDataMap;

    {
        CurrentPlotSettings = value switch
        {
            0 => CrossSectionSettings,
            1 => MacSettings,
            2 => LacSettings,
            3 => DepthsSettings,
            4 => ZeffSettings,
            5 => IsBuildupPlotActive ? BuildupSettings : ZeqSettings,
            _ => CrossSectionSettings
        };
    }

    partial void OnIsBuildupPlotActiveChanged(bool value)
    {
        if (SelectedMainTabIndex == 5) CurrentPlotSettings = value ? BuildupSettings : ZeqSettings;
        RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }
    #endregion

    #region Physics Parameters & Toggles

    [ObservableProperty] private bool isEbfMode = true;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool isEabfMode = false;

    partial void OnNValueChanged(double value) { TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); }

    partial void OnShowTripletChanged(bool value) { TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); }
    partial void OnShowPhotonuclearChanged(bool value) { TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); }

    partial void OnIsEbfModeChanged(bool value) { if (value) { SelectedBuildupType = BuildupFactorType.Exposure; IsBuildupPlotActive = true; TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); } }
    partial void OnIsEabfModeChanged(bool value) { if (value) { SelectedBuildupType = BuildupFactorType.EnergyAbsorption; IsBuildupPlotActive = true; TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); } }
    partial void OnIsZeqModeChanged(bool value) { if (value) { IsBuildupPlotActive = false; TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); } }

    partial void OnIsUserGridChanged(bool value) { TableVM.ClearCaches(); UpdateTables(); }
    partial void OnUserGridInputChanged(string value) { TableVM.ClearCaches(); UpdateTables(); }
    partial void OnTargetMfpInputChanged(string value)
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(TargetMfps));
        TableVM.ClearCaches();
        RefreshUIOnly();
    }

    public double[] TargetMfps
    {
        get
        {
            var parts = TargetMfpInput.Split(new[] { ',', ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            var mfps = new List<double>();
            foreach (var part in parts)
            {
                if (double.TryParse(part, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Any, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double val) && val >= 0) mfps.Add(val);
            }
            return mfps.Distinct().OrderBy(x => x).ToArray();
        }
    }
    #endregion

    #region Composition Inputs & Library Forwarding
    [ObservableProperty] private string materialNameInput = "New Material";
    [ObservableProperty] private string materialDensityInput = "";
    [ObservableProperty] private Element? selectedElementInput;
    [ObservableProperty] private string compoundFormulaInput = "H2O";
    [ObservableProperty] private string mixtureInput = "H2O 0.9\r\nNaCl 0.1";
    [ObservableProperty] private bool isMixtureByWeight = true;

    private string[] _densityMemories = new string[3] { "", "", "" };
    private string _mfpMemory = "";

    // NEW: Gets all materials currently checked for comparison
    // Passthrough properties to LibraryVM so XAML bindings remain unbroken
    public ObservableCollection<Material> Materials => LibraryVM.Materials;
    public bool HasMaterials => Materials.Count > 0;
    public IEnumerable<Material> ComparedMaterials => Materials.Where(m => m.IsCompared);

    public ObservableCollection<Material> MaterialLibrary => LibraryVM.MaterialLibrary;
    public ObservableCollection<Material> FilteredLibrary => LibraryVM.FilteredLibrary;
    public ObservableCollection<AbsorptionEdgeRow>? AbsorptionEdgesData => TableVM.AbsorptionEdgesData;
    public IRelayCommand CopyAbsorptionEdgesTableCommand => TableVM.CopyAbsorptionEdgesTableCommand;

    {
        get => LibraryVM.SelectedMaterial;
        set
        {
            LibraryVM.SelectedMaterial = value;
            OnPropertyChanged();

            // NEW: Tell the UI to evaluate the view state whenever a material is clicked or created
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowMainContent));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowEmptyState));

            OnSelectedMaterialChanged(value);
        }
    }
    public Material? SelectedLibraryMaterial
    {
        get => LibraryVM.SelectedLibraryMaterial;
        set { LibraryVM.SelectedLibraryMaterial = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
    }

    {
        get => LibraryVM.LibrarySearchText;
        set { LibraryVM.LibrarySearchText = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
    }

    {
        get => LibraryVM.ShowCustomMaterials;
        set { LibraryVM.ShowCustomMaterials = value; OnPropertyChanged(); }
    }

    public bool IsRenamingCustomMaterial
    {
        get => LibraryVM.IsRenamingCustomMaterial;
        set { LibraryVM.IsRenamingCustomMaterial = value; OnPropertyChanged(); OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsNotRenamingCustomMaterial)); }
    }
    public bool IsNotRenamingCustomMaterial => !IsRenamingCustomMaterial;

    public bool HasSelectedLibraryMaterial => SelectedLibraryMaterial != null;
    public bool CanDeleteMaterial => SelectedMaterial != null;
    public bool ShowMainContent => SelectedMaterial != null || IsCompareMode;
    public bool ShowEmptyState => !ShowMainContent;
    public bool IsElementSelected => SelectedElementInput != null;
    public bool HasValidDensity => IsCompareMode
            ? ComparedMaterials.Any(m => m.Density > 0)
            : SelectedMaterial?.Density > 0;

    public bool HasBuildupFactors => IsCompareMode
        ? ComparedMaterials.Any(m => BuildupCalc.GetGpParameters(m, 1.0, BuildupFactorType.Exposure) != null)
        : SelectedMaterial != null && BuildupCalc.GetGpParameters(SelectedMaterial, 1.0, BuildupFactorType.Exposure) != null;

    partial void OnSelectedInputTabIndexChanging(int value)
    {
        if (!IsLoadMode && SelectedInputTabIndex >= 0 && SelectedInputTabIndex < 3)
            _densityMemories[SelectedInputTabIndex] = MaterialDensityInput;
    }
    partial void OnSelectedInputTabIndexChanged(int value)
    {
        if (!IsLoadMode && value >= 0 && value < 3) MaterialDensityInput = _densityMemories[value];

        // ADD THIS: Track if we are on the Mixture tab (Index 2)
        IsMixtureMode = (value == 2);

        UpdateMaterialName();
    }
    partial void OnSelectedLoadTabIndexChanged(int value)
    {
        // 0 = Standard Library, 1 = Custom Materials
        ShowCustomMaterials = (value == 1);
    }

    partial void OnCompoundFormulaInputChanged(string value) => UpdateMaterialName();
    partial void OnMixtureInputChanged(string value) => UpdateMaterialName();
    partial void OnMaterialDensityInputChanged(string value) => UpdateMaterialName();
    partial void OnSelectedElementInputChanged(Element? value) { OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsElementSelected)); UpdateMaterialName(); }

    partial void OnIsLoadModeChanged(bool value)
    {
        if (value)
        {
            MaterialDensityInput = SelectedLibraryMaterial?.Density?.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "";

            // ADD THIS: Force the table to populate when the Load drawer opens
            UpdatePreviewCompositionTable();
        }
        else if (SelectedInputTabIndex >= 0 && SelectedInputTabIndex < 3)
        {
            MaterialDensityInput = _densityMemories[SelectedInputTabIndex];
        }
        UpdateMaterialName();
    }

    public MainViewModel()
    {
        // Resolve Dependencies from App.Services
        var services = App.Current.Services;
        _ptService = services.GetService<PeriodicTableService>()!;
        BuildupCalc = services.GetService<BuildupCalculator>()!;

        LibraryVM = services.GetService<MaterialLibraryViewModel>()!;
        ChartVM = services.GetService<ChartViewModel>()!;
        TableVM = services.GetService<DataTableViewModel>()!;

        // Wire up sub-ViewModel events to propagate to MainWindow.xaml.cs
        ChartVM.RequestRepaint += (s, e) => RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
        LibraryVM.ShowAlert += (msg, title) => ShowAlert?.Invoke(msg, title);
        LibraryVM.RequestConfirmation += (title, msg, btnText) => RequestConfirmation?.Invoke(title, msg, btnText) ?? Task.FromResult(false);

        // Listen for changes in the LibraryVM so the UI stays dynamically synced
        LibraryVM.PropertyChanged += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(LibraryVM.SelectedLibraryMaterial))
            {
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(SelectedLibraryMaterial));
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasSelectedLibraryMaterial));

                if (IsLoadMode)
                {
                    MaterialDensityInput = LibraryVM.SelectedLibraryMaterial?.Density?.ToString(System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture) ?? "";
                    UpdateMaterialName();
                    UpdatePreviewCompositionTable();
                }
            }
            // Keep the Load Tab index synced with the underlying library filter
            else if (e.PropertyName == nameof(LibraryVM.ShowCustomMaterials))
            {
                OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ShowCustomMaterials));
                // If ShowCustomMaterials is true, switch to tab 1. Otherwise, tab 0.
                SelectedLoadTabIndex = LibraryVM.ShowCustomMaterials ? 1 : 0;
            }
        };

        Materials.CollectionChanged += (s, e) =>
        {
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasMaterials));

            // If they delete the last material while comparing, turn compare mode off
            if (Materials.Count == 0 && IsCompareMode)
            {
                IsCompareMode = false;
            }
        };
    }

    public async Task InitializeDataAsync()
    {
        IsLoading = true; // Show your progress ring

        await Task.Run(() =>
        {
            string appBase = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;

            // This runs on a background thread, reading all the CSVs without freezing the window
            _ptService.LoadData(
                System.IO.Path.Combine(appBase, "Data", "Isotopes.csv"),
                System.IO.Path.Combine(appBase, "Data", "Elements.csv")
            );
        });

        // Code below this line automatically returns to the UI thread

        // Setup Initial UI State now that data is loaded
        foreach (var e in _ptService.GetAllElements())
        {
            AvailableElements.Add(e);
        }

        // ⬇️ PASTE THE JSON LOADING HERE ⬇️
        List<string> allLibraryFiles = new List<string>();

        string appBasePath = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.BaseDirectory;
        string defaultDataFolder = System.IO.Path.Combine(appBasePath, "Data");
        if (System.IO.Directory.Exists(defaultDataFolder))
        {
            allLibraryFiles.AddRange(System.IO.Directory.GetFiles(defaultDataFolder, "*.json"));
        }

        string appData = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData);
        string userDataFolder = System.IO.Path.Combine(appData, "Panex", "Data");
        if (System.IO.Directory.Exists(userDataFolder))
        {
            allLibraryFiles.AddRange(System.IO.Directory.GetFiles(userDataFolder, "*.json"));
        }

        if (allLibraryFiles.Count > 0)
        {
            LibraryVM.LoadLibraries(allLibraryFiles);
        }
        // ⬆️ --------------------------- ⬆️

        SelectedElementInput = AvailableElements.FirstOrDefault(e => e.Symbol == "H");
        CurrentPlotSettings = CrossSectionSettings;
        UpdateMaterialName();

        IsLoading = false; // Hide your progress ring
    }

    private void UpdatePreviewCompositionTable()
    {
        PreviewCompositionTable.Clear();
        if (SelectedLibraryMaterial == null) return;

        foreach (var kvp in SelectedLibraryMaterial.Composition)
        {
            PreviewCompositionTable.Add(new PreviewRow
            {
                Symbol = kvp.Key.Symbol,
                // CHANGED: P4 formats as a percentage with 4 decimal places
                FractionText = kvp.Value.ToString("P4")
            });
        }
    }
    #endregion

    #region UI & Interaction Logic
    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task CreateMaterial()
    {
        await Task.Delay(200); // UI Yield for Drawer Animation

        double? density = null;
        if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(MaterialDensityInput))
        {
            if (double.TryParse(MaterialDensityInput, out double d)) density = d;
            else { ShowAlert?.Invoke("Density must be a valid number.", "Error"); return; }
        }

        try
        {
            Material? newMat = null;
            if (IsLoadMode && SelectedLibraryMaterial != null)
            {
                newMat = new Material
                {
                    Name = SelectedLibraryMaterial.Name,
                    Density = density ?? SelectedLibraryMaterial.Density,
                    Composition = SelectedLibraryMaterial.Composition.ToDictionary(k => k.Key, v => v.Value)
                };
            }
            else
            {
                if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 0 && SelectedElementInput != null) newMat = _ptService.CreateFromElement(MaterialNameInput, SelectedElementInput, density);
                else if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 1) newMat = _ptService.CreateFromCompound(MaterialNameInput, CompoundFormulaInput, density);
                else if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 2) newMat = _ptService.CreateFromMixture(MaterialNameInput, MixtureInput, density, IsMixtureByWeight);
            }

            if (newMat != null)
            {
                Materials.Add(newMat);
                SelectedMaterial = newMat;
                MaterialCreated?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowAlert?.Invoke($"Error:\n{ex.Message}", "Error"); }
    }

    public void SortLibrary(string columnName, bool ascending) => LibraryVM.SortLibrary(columnName, ascending);

    public bool IsAllIsotopesSelected
    {
        get => IsotopeOptions.Count > 0 && IsotopeOptions.All(i => i.IsSelected);
        set { if (IsAllIsotopesSelected == value) return; _suppressRefresh = true; foreach (var item in IsotopeOptions) item.IsSelected = value; _suppressRefresh = false; OnPropertyChanged(); TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); }
    }

    public bool IsAllSubshellsSelected
    {
        get => SubshellOptions.Count > 0 && SubshellOptions.All(s => s.IsSelected);
        set { if (IsAllSubshellsSelected == value) return; _suppressRefresh = true; foreach (var item in SubshellOptions) item.IsSelected = value; _suppressRefresh = false; OnPropertyChanged(); TableVM.ClearCaches(); RefreshUIOnly(); }
    }

    private void OnItemChanged()
    {
        if (_suppressRefresh) return;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsAllIsotopesSelected));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsAllSubshellsSelected));
        TableVM.ClearCaches();
        RefreshUIOnly();
    }

    // NEW: Command bound to checkboxes in the UI to trigger a chart redraw
    [RelayCommand]
    private void ToggleMaterialCompare()
    {
        if (IsCompareMode)
        {
            // NEW: Tell the tabs to check if they should hide/show based on the new mix of checked materials
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasValidDensity));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasBuildupFactors));

            RefreshUIOnly();
        }
    }

    private bool _suppressRefresh = false;
    private System.Threading.CancellationTokenSource? _refreshCts;

    private void OnSelectedMaterialChanged(Material? value)
    {
        if (value == null)
        {
            IsotopeOptions.Clear();
            SubshellOptions.Clear();
            TableVM.ClearCaches();

            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanDeleteMaterial));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasValidDensity));
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasBuildupFactors));
            DeleteMaterialCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();

            RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            return;
        }

        IsLoading = true;
        _ = ProcessNewMaterialUIAsync();
    }

    private void ValidateCurrentTab()
    {
        if ((SelectedMainTabIndex == 2 || SelectedMainTabIndex == 3) && !HasValidDensity)
        {
            SelectedMainTabIndex = 0;
        }
        else if (SelectedMainTabIndex == 5 && !HasBuildupFactors)
        {
            SelectedMainTabIndex = 0;
        }
    }

    private async Task ProcessNewMaterialUIAsync()
    {
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CanDeleteMaterial));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasValidDensity));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasBuildupFactors));
        DeleteMaterialCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();

        ValidateCurrentTab();

        await Task.Delay(50);
        PopulateDetailedOptions();
        UpdateCompositionTable();

        await RefreshAllAsync();
    }

    private void RefreshAll() => _ = RefreshAllAsync();

    private async Task RefreshAllAsync()
    {
        if (_suppressRefresh) return;
        _refreshCts?.Cancel();
        _refreshCts = new System.Threading.CancellationTokenSource();
        var token = _refreshCts.Token;

        var currentMaterial = SelectedMaterial;

        // FIX: Allow refresh to continue if we are in Compare Mode so tables update
        if (currentMaterial == null && !IsCompareMode) { IsLoading = false; RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty); return; }

        IsLoading = true;
        try
        {
            await Task.Run(() =>
            {
                if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

                // Only do the heavy pre-calc if we have a single material focused
                if (currentMaterial != null)
                {
                    currentMaterial.GetTotalCrossSectionCurve();
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    currentMaterial.GetEffectiveAtomicNumberCurve();
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    currentMaterial.GetPhotonuclearCrossSectionCurve();
                    if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;
                    if (currentMaterial.Density > 0) currentMaterial.GetTotalMassAttenuationCurve();
                }
            }, token);

            if (token.IsCancellationRequested) return;

            // --- NEW PLACEMENT FOR ABSORPTION EDGES ---
            // Always update the global Absorption Edges flyout, regardless of the active tab
            if (IsCompareMode)
            {
                TableVM.AbsorptionEdgesData = null;
            }
            else if (currentMaterial != null)
            {
                TableVM.UpdateAbsorptionEdgesTable(currentMaterial);
            }
            OnPropertyChanged(nameof(AbsorptionEdgesData));
            // ------------------------------------------

            if (ShowGrid) UpdateTables();

            RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
            await Task.Delay(50, token);
        }
        catch (TaskCanceledException) { }
        catch (Exception ex) { ShowAlert?.Invoke($"Error calculating material data:\n{ex.Message}", "Calculation Error"); }
        finally { if (!token.IsCancellationRequested) IsLoading = false; }
    }

    private void RefreshUIOnly()
    {
        if (_suppressRefresh) return;
        if (ShowGrid) UpdateTables();
        RequestRepaint?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
    }

    public void ChangeDatabaseLibrary(string libraryType)
    {
        IsLoading = true;

        // 1. Tell the service to swap the element data
        _ptService.SwitchPhotoatomicLibrary(libraryType);

        // 2. Clear caches on all loaded materials so they are forced to recalculate
        foreach (var mat in Materials) mat.ClearCaches();
        foreach (var mat in MaterialLibrary) mat.ClearCaches();

        // 3. Clear the UI tables
        TableVM.ClearCaches();

        // ADD THIS: Re-evaluate the active material against the new library 
        // to instantly hide/show the subshell filter button!
        PopulateDetailedOptions();

        // 4. Force the charts and tables to redraw with the new data
        RefreshAll();
    }

    private void PopulateDetailedOptions()
    {
        IsotopeOptions.Clear();
        SubshellOptions.Clear();
        if (SelectedMaterial == null) return;

        foreach (var kvp in SelectedMaterial.Composition)
        {
            foreach (var iso in kvp.Key.Isotopes.Where(i => i.PhotonuclearData != null))
                IsotopeOptions.Add(new SelectableItem($"{kvp.Key.Symbol}-{iso.A} (PN)", OnItemChanged) { Z = iso.Z, A = iso.A, IsIsotope = true });

            foreach (var p in kvp.Key.PhotoatomicData.Where(x => x.C == 73 && x.S == 91))
            {
                double edgeKeV = p.Energies.FirstOrDefault() * 1000.0;
                string displayName = $"{kvp.Key.Symbol} {p.SubshellName} ({edgeKeV:F2} keV)";
                SubshellOptions.Add(new SelectableItem(displayName, OnItemChanged) { Z = kvp.Key.Z, SubshellIndex = p.X, IsSubshell = true });
            }
        }

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsAllIsotopesSelected));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsAllSubshellsSelected));

        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasSubshells));
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyIsotopes()
    {
        if (SelectedElementInput == null || SelectedElementInput.Isotopes.Count == 0) return;

        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("A\tMass (u)\tAbundance");

        foreach (var iso in SelectedElementInput.Isotopes)
        {
            sb.AppendLine($"{iso.A}\t{iso.Mass:F5}\t{iso.Abundance:P3}");
        }

        var dp = new Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.DataPackage();
        dp.SetText(sb.ToString());
        Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.Clipboard.SetContent(dp);
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyPreviewComposition()
    {
        if (PreviewCompositionTable == null || PreviewCompositionTable.Count == 0) return;

        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("Symbol\tFraction");

        foreach (var row in PreviewCompositionTable)
        {
            sb.AppendLine($"{row.Symbol}\t{row.FractionText}");
        }

        var dp = new Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.DataPackage();
        dp.SetText(sb.ToString());
        Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.Clipboard.SetContent(dp);
    }

    #endregion

    #region Plotting & Table Routing Logic
    // Plotting methods delegate strictly to ChartVM and dynamically branch based on Compare Mode
    public void PlotTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotCrossSectionComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
        else ChartVM.PlotCrossSectionTo(plot, SelectedMaterial, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
    }

    public void PlotMacTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotMacComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
        else ChartVM.PlotMacTo(plot, SelectedMaterial, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
    }

    public void PlotLacTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotLacComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
        else ChartVM.PlotLacTo(plot, SelectedMaterial, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
    }

    public void PlotDepthTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotDepthComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, NValue, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
        else ChartVM.PlotDepthTo(plot, SelectedMaterial, NValue, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
    }

    public void PlotZeffTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotZeffComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, CompareUseInterpolatedZeff, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
        else ChartVM.PlotZeffTo(plot, SelectedMaterial);
    }

    public void PlotZeqTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotZeqComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials);
        else ChartVM.PlotZeqTo(plot, SelectedMaterial);
    }

    public void PlotBuildupTo(ScottPlot.Plot plot)
    {
        if (IsCompareMode) ChartVM.PlotBuildupComparisonTo(plot, ComparedMaterials, TargetMfps.FirstOrDefault(), SelectedBuildupType);
        else ChartVM.PlotBuildupTo(plot, SelectedMaterial, TargetMfps, SelectedBuildupType);
    }

    private List<double> GetEnergyGrid()
    {
        List<double> energies = new();

        if (IsUserGrid)
        {
            foreach (var p in UserGridInput.Split(new[] { ',', ' ', '\t', '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries))
                if (double.TryParse(p, System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out double e)) energies.Add(e);
        }
        else if (SelectedMaterial != null) energies.AddRange(SelectedMaterial.GetMasterEnergyGrid());

        return energies.Where(e => e >= 0.001).Distinct().OrderBy(e => e).ToList();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void UpdateTables()
    {
        var energies = GetEnergyGrid();

        if (IsCompareMode)
        {
            TableVM.UpdateCrossSectionComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, energies, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateMacComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, energies, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateLacComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, energies, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateDepthsComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, energies, NValue, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateZeffComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, energies, CompareUseInterpolatedZeff, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateBuildupComparisonTable(ComparedMaterials, TargetMfps.FirstOrDefault(), SelectedBuildupType);
        }
        else
        {
            if (SelectedMaterial == null) return;

            TableVM.UpdateCrossSectionDataTable(SelectedMaterial, energies, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
            TableVM.UpdateMacDataTable(SelectedMaterial, energies, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
            TableVM.UpdateLacDataTable(SelectedMaterial, energies, ShowTotal, ShowCoherent, ShowIncoherent, ShowPhotoelectric, ShowPair, ShowTriplet, ShowPhotonuclear, IsotopeOptions, SubshellOptions);
            TableVM.UpdateDepthsDataTable(SelectedMaterial, energies, NValue, DepthIncludePhotoelectric, DepthIncludeCoherent, DepthIncludeIncoherent, DepthIncludePair, DepthIncludeTriplet, DepthIncludePhotonuclear);
            TableVM.UpdateZeffDataTable(SelectedMaterial, energies);
            TableVM.UpdateBuildupDataTable(SelectedMaterial, TargetMfps, SelectedBuildupType);
        }

        // --- EXISTING DATA NOTIFICATIONS ---
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CrossSectionData));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(MacData));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LacData));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DepthsData));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ZeffData));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(BuildupData));

        // --- HEADER NOTIFICATIONS ---
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(CrossSectionHeaders));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(MacHeaders));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(LacHeaders));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(DepthsHeaders));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(ZeffHeaders));
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(BuildupHeaders));
    }

    private void UpdateMaterialName()
    {
        if (IsLoadMode) { MaterialNameInput = SelectedLibraryMaterial?.Name ?? "Select a Material"; return; }

        string baseName = "New Material";

        if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 0) baseName = SelectedElementInput != null ? $"{SelectedElementInput.Name} ({SelectedElementInput.Symbol})" : "Select an Element";
        else if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 1) baseName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(CompoundFormulaInput) ? "New Compound" : CompoundFormulaInput;
        else if (SelectedInputTabIndex == 2)
        {
            int count = MixtureInput.Split(new[] { '\r', '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Length;
            baseName = count > 0 ? $"Mixture ({count} components)" : "New Mixture";
        }

        MaterialNameInput = baseName;
    }

    private void UpdateCompositionTable()
    {
        CompositionTable.Clear();
        if (SelectedMaterial == null) return;

        double totalMass = SelectedMaterial.Composition.Sum(x => x.Key.AtomicMass * x.Value);

        foreach (var kvp in SelectedMaterial.Composition)
        {
            CompositionTable.Add(new CompositionRow
            {
                Z = kvp.Key.Z,
                Symbol = kvp.Key.Symbol,
                Name = kvp.Key.Name,
                AtomicMass = kvp.Key.AtomicMass,
                AtomFraction = kvp.Value,
                MassFraction = (kvp.Key.AtomicMass * kvp.Value) / totalMass
            });
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CopyComposition()
    {
        if (CompositionTable == null || CompositionTable.Count == 0) return;

        var sb = new System.Text.StringBuilder();
        sb.AppendLine("Z\tSymbol\tElement\tAtomic Mass (u)\tAtom %\tMass %");

        foreach (var row in CompositionTable) sb.AppendLine($"{row.Z}\t{row.Symbol}\t{row.Name}\t{row.AtomicMassString}\t{row.AtomFractionString}\t{row.MassFractionString}");

        var dp = new Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.DataPackage();
        dp.SetText(sb.ToString());
        Windows.ApplicationModel.DataTransfer.Clipboard.SetContent(dp);
    }
    #endregion
} 
===== ..\Panex\ViewModels\MaterialLibraryViewModel.cs =====
﻿// File: \ViewModels\MaterialLibraryViewModel.cs
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Panex.Models;
using Panex.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Panex.ViewModels;

public partial class MaterialLibraryViewModel : ObservableObject
{
    private readonly PeriodicTableService _ptService;

    public event Action<string, string>? ShowAlert;
    public event Func<string, string, string, Task<bool>>? RequestConfirmation;
    public event EventHandler? MaterialCreated;

    [ObservableProperty] private Material? selectedMaterial;
    [ObservableProperty] private Material? selectedLibraryMaterial;
    [ObservableProperty] private string librarySearchText = "";
    [ObservableProperty] private bool showCustomMaterials = false;
    [ObservableProperty] private bool isRenamingCustomMaterial = false;

    public ObservableCollection<Material> Materials { get; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<Material> MaterialLibrary { get; } = new();
    [ObservableProperty] private ObservableCollection<Material> filteredLibrary = new();

    private string _currentSortColumn = "Name";
    private bool _isSortAscending = true;
    private string _originalCustomMaterialName = "";

    public bool IsNotRenamingCustomMaterial => !IsRenamingCustomMaterial;
    public bool HasSelectedLibraryMaterial => SelectedLibraryMaterial != null;
    public bool CanDeleteMaterial => SelectedMaterial != null;

    public MaterialLibraryViewModel(PeriodicTableService ptService)
    {
        _ptService = ptService;
    }

    partial void OnLibrarySearchTextChanged(string value) => ApplyLibraryFilter();
    partial void OnShowCustomMaterialsChanged(bool value) => ApplyLibraryFilter();
    partial void OnIsRenamingCustomMaterialChanged(bool value) => OnPropertyChanged(nameof(IsNotRenamingCustomMaterial));

    partial void OnSelectedLibraryMaterialChanged(Material? value)
    {
        IsRenamingCustomMaterial = false;
        OnPropertyChanged(nameof(HasSelectedLibraryMaterial));
    }

    public void LoadLibraries(IEnumerable<string> jsonFilePaths)
    {
        foreach (var file in jsonFilePaths)
        {
            foreach (var mat in _ptService.LoadMaterialsFromJson(file))
            {
                MaterialLibrary.Add(mat);
            }
        }
        ApplyLibraryFilter();
    }

    public void SortLibrary(string columnName, bool ascending)
    {
        _currentSortColumn = columnName;
        _isSortAscending = ascending;
        ApplyLibraryFilter();
    }

    private void ApplyLibraryFilter()
    {
        var tempList = MaterialLibrary.Where(m =>
            (m.Category == "My Custom Materials" == ShowCustomMaterials) &&
            (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(LibrarySearchText) || m.Name.Contains(LibrarySearchText, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        ).ToList();

        IEnumerable<Material> sortedList = _currentSortColumn switch
        {
            "Category" => _isSortAscending ? tempList.OrderBy(m => m.Category).ThenBy(m => m.Name) : tempList.OrderByDescending(m => m.Category).ThenBy(m => m.Name),
            "Density" => _isSortAscending ? tempList.OrderBy(m => m.Density ?? 0) : tempList.OrderByDescending(m => m.Density ?? 0),
            "Reference" => _isSortAscending ? tempList.OrderBy(m => m.Reference).ThenBy(m => m.Name) : tempList.OrderByDescending(m => m.Reference).ThenBy(m => m.Name),
            _ => _isSortAscending ? tempList.OrderBy(m => m.Name) : tempList.OrderByDescending(m => m.Name),
        };

        FilteredLibrary.Clear();
        foreach (var m in sortedList) FilteredLibrary.Add(m);
        if (SelectedLibraryMaterial == null || !FilteredLibrary.Contains(SelectedLibraryMaterial))
            SelectedLibraryMaterial = FilteredLibrary.FirstOrDefault();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void DeleteMaterial(Material? materialToDelete)
    {
        var target = materialToDelete ?? SelectedMaterial;
        if (target != null)
        {
            Materials.Remove(target);
            if (SelectedMaterial == target) SelectedMaterial = null;
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task SaveMaterialToLibrary(Material? materialToSave)
    {
        var target = materialToSave ?? SelectedMaterial;
        if (target == null) return;

        try
        {
            target.Category = "My Custom Materials";
            target.Reference = "Material_User";

            // Check if it already exists
            var existing = MaterialLibrary.FirstOrDefault(m => m.Name == target.Name && m.Category == "My Custom Materials");
            if (existing != null)
            {
                // Ask for permission before obliterating their old material
                if (RequestConfirmation != null)
                {
                    // FIXED: Using 'target' and the Overwrite message
                    bool confirm = await RequestConfirmation.Invoke("Overwrite Material", $"A custom material named '{target.Name}' already exists. Do you want to overwrite it?", "Overwrite");
                    if (!confirm) return; // User canceled the save
                }

                MaterialLibrary.Remove(existing);
            }

            MaterialLibrary.Add(target);
            ForceSaveCustomLibrary();
            LibrarySearchText = "";
            ShowCustomMaterials = true;
            ApplyLibraryFilter();

            ShowAlert?.Invoke($"'{target.Name}' saved to your custom library!", "Success");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            ShowAlert?.Invoke($"Could not save material:\n{ex.Message}", "Error");
        }
    }
    public void ForceSaveCustomLibrary()
    {
        // Route to C:\Users\[User]\AppData\Local\Panex\Data\
        string appData = Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData);
        string userFilePath = System.IO.Path.Combine(appData, "Panex", "Data", "Material_User.json");

        // Ensure the directory exists before attempting to write
        string? directory = System.IO.Path.GetDirectoryName(userFilePath);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(directory) && !System.IO.Directory.Exists(directory))
        {
            System.IO.Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
        }

        var customMaterials = MaterialLibrary.Where(m => m.Category == "My Custom Materials");
        _ptService.SaveMaterialsToJson(customMaterials, userFilePath);
        ApplyLibraryFilter();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void BeginRenameCustomMaterial()
    {
        if (SelectedLibraryMaterial?.Category == "My Custom Materials")
        {
            _originalCustomMaterialName = SelectedLibraryMaterial.Name;
            IsRenamingCustomMaterial = true;
        }
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void CancelRenameCustomMaterial()
    {
        if (SelectedLibraryMaterial != null) SelectedLibraryMaterial.Name = _originalCustomMaterialName;
        IsRenamingCustomMaterial = false;
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private async Task DeleteLibraryMaterial(Material mat)
    {
        if (mat == null || mat.Category != "My Custom Materials") return;
        if (RequestConfirmation != null)
        {
            // FIXED: Using 'mat' and the Delete message
            bool confirm = await RequestConfirmation.Invoke("Delete Custom Material", $"Are you sure you want to permanently delete '{mat.Name}'? This action cannot be undone.", "Delete");
            if (!confirm) return;
        }

        // Don't forget to actually remove the material if confirmed!
        MaterialLibrary.Remove(mat);
        ForceSaveCustomLibrary();
    }

    [RelayCommand]
    private void SaveCustomMaterialName()
    {
        IsRenamingCustomMaterial = false;
        ForceSaveCustomLibrary();
    }
} 
===== ..\Panex\ViewModels\PlotSettings.cs =====
﻿// File: \ViewModels\PlotSettings.cs
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace Panex.ViewModels
{
    public partial class PlotSettings : ObservableObject
    {
        private readonly Action _repaint;

        // Baseline defaults to remember for the Reset command
        private readonly bool _defaultIsXLog;
        private readonly bool _defaultIsYLog;
        private bool _defaultShowMinorGrid;

        [ObservableProperty] private bool isXLog;
        [ObservableProperty] private bool isYLog;
        [ObservableProperty] private bool showMajorGrid = true;
        [ObservableProperty] private bool showMinorGrid;

        [ObservableProperty] private bool useCustomXLimits;
        [ObservableProperty] private double xMin = 0.001;
        [ObservableProperty] private double xMax = 100000.0;

        [ObservableProperty] private bool useCustomYLimits;
        [ObservableProperty] private double yMin = 0.0001;
        [ObservableProperty] private double yMax = 10000.0;

        public ObservableCollection<CurveStyle> CurveStyles { get; } = new();

        public PlotSettings(bool isXLog, bool isYLog, Action repaint)
        {
            _repaint = repaint;

            _defaultIsXLog = isXLog;
            _defaultIsYLog = isYLog;

            IsXLog = isXLog;
            IsYLog = isYLog;
        }

        // Call this immediately after object initialization in MainViewModel 
        // so it captures the specific minor grid state for that specific plot.
        public void CaptureDefaults()
        {
            _defaultShowMinorGrid = ShowMinorGrid;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ResetToDefaults()
        {
            // Revert axes and grids to the captured baseline
            IsXLog = _defaultIsXLog;
            IsYLog = _defaultIsYLog;
            ShowMinorGrid = _defaultShowMinorGrid;
            ShowMajorGrid = true;

            // Revert custom axis limits
            UseCustomXLimits = false;
            UseCustomYLimits = false;

            // Revert all active curves (colors, line widths, patterns)
            foreach (var style in CurveStyles)
            {
                style.ResetToDefaults();
            }
        }

        // Trigger the repaint debounce timer whenever a visual property changes
        partial void OnIsXLogChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnIsYLogChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnShowMajorGridChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnShowMinorGridChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnUseCustomXLimitsChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnXMinChanged(double value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnXMaxChanged(double value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnUseCustomYLimitsChanged(bool value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnYMinChanged(double value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
        partial void OnYMaxChanged(double value) => _repaint?.Invoke();
    }
}

===== ..\Panex\App.xaml.cs =====
﻿// File: \App.xaml.cs
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
using Panex.Services;
using Panex.ViewModels;
using System;

namespace Panex
{
    public partial class App : Application
    {
        private Window? m_window;

        // Expose the ServiceProvider so ViewModels created by XAML can resolve dependencies
        public IServiceProvider Services { get; }
        public new static App Current => (App)Application.Current;

        public App()
        {
            this.InitializeComponent();
            Services = ConfigureServices();
        }

        private IServiceProvider ConfigureServices()
        {
            var services = new ServiceCollection();

            // 1. Core Services
            services.AddSingleton<PeriodicTableService>();
            services.AddSingleton<BuildupCalculator>();

            services.AddSingleton<PhotoatomicDataService>(); // EPDL Reader
            services.AddSingleton<XcomDataService>();        // XCOM Reader

            // 2. ViewModels
            services.AddTransient<MaterialLibraryViewModel>();
            services.AddTransient<ChartViewModel>();
            services.AddTransient<DataTableViewModel>();

            // We just build the provider and return it. No blocking data loads here!
            return services.BuildServiceProvider();
        }

        protected override void OnLaunched(Microsoft.UI.Xaml.LaunchActivatedEventArgs args)
        {
            m_window = new MainWindow();
            m_window.Activate();
        }
    }
}
